**21 October 2025**

**1\. Run D1,D2,D3 nucleosome samples (no treatment) on gel**

5 uL each of the D1, D2, D3 samples will be run on a 0.5% agarose gel & stained with SYBR Green dye.

**2\. Viral titer for Gclav1, GCDv1-1, GCDv1-2 & HCDv1**

- (Optional) Dilute lentiviral supernatant in fresh culture medium and keep the final volume of 1 mL for each sample.  Include culture medium as a negative control. Note: For unknown samples, we recommend several dilutions for each sample.   
- Add 10 μL of ViraBind™ Lentivirus Reagent A (100X) to 1 mL of lentiviral sample, and mix by inverting.  Immediately add 10 μL of ViraBind™ Lentivirus Reagent B (100X) and mix by inverting.  Incubate 30 minutes at 37ºC.   
- Centrifuge 5 minutes at 12,000 rpm.  Carefully remove the supernatant and dissolve the pellet in 250 μL of Sample Diluent.  Vortex well and incubate 30 minutes at 37ºC to inactivate the viruses.   
- Add 100 µL of inactivated lentiviral sample or p24 antigen standard to anti-p24 antibody coated plate.  
- Cover with a Plate Cover and incubate at 4ºC overnight.

**3\. Isolate gDNA from 293TX cells & fragment to 20-kb**

Prepare two tubes with 15 ug of DNA each \[150 uL total volume in TE buffer\]

**![][image1]**

Load the gTubes into the centrifuge and spin for 60 sec at 5800 rpm. Then invert the tube and spin again at 5800 rpm for 60 sec.

Place the gTube on the UNLOAD position and collect the sheared sample from the screw cap.

The sample post fragmentation was run on the SpeedVac to obtain a highly concentrated sample → becomes easier to use for reconstitution experiments.

Run some of the sample on a 0.5% agarose gel to check for the integrity and shear size of the sample.

**3\. Hia5 treatment of nucleosome samples**

Set up reactions for the following samples:

D1

* 5 uL sample \+ Nuc buffer C (13 uL) \+ 0.5 uL SAM \+ 1 uL 20X Spermidine \+ 0.5 uL Hia5 (6 reactions)

D2

* 5 uL sample \+ Nuc buffer C (13 uL) \+ 0.5 uL SAM \+ 1 uL 20X Spermidine \+ 0.5 uL Hia5 (6 reactions)

B3

* 5 uL sample \+ Nuc buffer C (13 uL) \+ 0.5 uL SAM \+ 1 uL 20X Spermidine \+ 0.5 uL Hia5 (1 reaction)

B4

* 5 uL sample \+ Nuc buffer C (13 uL) \+ 0.5 uL SAM \+ 1 uL 20X Spermidine \+ 0.5 uL Hia5 (1 reaction)

| 37 C | 1 hour |
| :---- | :---- |
| 80 C | 10 mins |

After Hia5 treatment, add 1 uL 10% SDS & 0.5 uL Proteinase K (20 mg/mL) in 20 uL reactions and incubate 30-60 mins, RT.

Perform clean-up with Ampure XP beads:

- Add 1.5X volume of AmpureXP beads and mix thoroughly. Incubate at RT for 15 mins.  
- Place tubes on magnetic rack and allow liquid to separate from beads for 5 mins.  
- Perform two washes with 80% ethanol (200-300 uL each)  
- Allow the beads to dry for \~30s and then add fresh TE buffer in desired volume for elution. Mix thoroughly and incubate at RT for 5 mins.  
- Place tubes on magnetic rack and allow liquid to separate from beads for 5 mins. Collect separated supernatant as purified DNA sample.

**4\. Large scale reconstitution of lig20K & lig50K**

*293T-20K \[X2\]*

Use 3.89 ug of DNA \[3.76 uL \+ 11.24 uL TE buffer\]

lig20K (D1) ⇒ 15 uL ⇒ 15 nM \[at 2 M salt concentration\]

Octamer molarity acc. to 60:1 ratio ⇒ 0.9 uM ⇒ 1.96 ug in 5 uL ⇒ Prepare 12 uL of stock with 500 ng/uL with refolding buffer \[1.11 uL stock \+ 8.89 uL refolding buffer\] \[O500\] ⇒ Prepare 5 uL octamer stock \[3.92 uL \+ 1.08 uL refolding buffer \+ glycerol\] \[1.78 M\] ⇒ Final concentration of mix \[1.95 M\]

Prepare the \[refolding buffer \+ glycerol\] fresh every time, just before use.

*High salt RB (2 L)*  
298.2 g KCl  
20 mL 1 M Tris-HCl, pH 7.5  
4 mL 0.5 M EDTA  
2 mL DTT

*Low salt RB (2 L)*  
37.28 g KCl  
20 mL 1 M Tris-HCl, pH 7.5  
4 mL 0.5 M EDTA  
2 mL DTT

Resuspend 1.54 g of DTT in 10 mL of water to prepare a 1 M stock if needed.

This trial is being run at 1.5 mL/min (pump 1\) & 1.3 mL/min (pump 2\)

**8\. D4, D5 recon lower salt dialysis**

500 mL

| 1 M Tris pH 8 | 7.5 mL |
| :---- | :---- |
| EDTA (0.5 M) | 1 mL |
| EGTA (0.5 M) | 500 uL |
| KCl (4 M) | 10 mL |
| TCEP | 5 mL |
| Glycerol | 25 mL |

Allow this buffer to mix and then use vacuum to purify the buffer. Store buffer.

Take 3 mini-dialysis columns labeled D4, D4 and D5 and then place them on top of the buffer solution for equilibration.

After equilibration, transfer the original reconstituted samples in D1, D2, D3 to each of the new mini dialysis columns. During the transfer, rinse the original columns with a little of the Buffer C and add this to the transferred samples undergoing buffer exchange. Leave this system O/N at 4 degrees.

**2\. *Hia5* treatment for D4, D5 and D6 recon problems**

D4 and D5-1 samples contained some level of the refolding buffer by mistake during the post salt exchange dialysis clean-up.

If these samples showed good results in the MNase assay, then this extra salt has not created significant issues.

*Estimating the salt levels*

D4recon \~ 100 uL volume with \~20 uL refolding buffer ⇒ I estimate the salt concentration to be \~450 mM at this point in the mix

For D4 based on the concentration I estimate using about 200 ng DNA per Hia5 reaction, this would be 7.2 uL per reaction. After making up this reaction completely, the salt concentration would be \~200 mM.

I am just going to dialyse D4 and D5-1 again 2-3 times with 15 mL each of nucleosome buffer C to ensure that the salt level goes back down to 80 mM before Hia5 treatment.

**4\. Optimisation of ligN40w107 PCR**

- Place tubes on magnetic rack and allow liquid to separate from beads for 5 mins. Collect separated supernatant as purified DNA sample.

***3\. D7 & D8 reconstitution sample testing***

Collect the salt exchange samples and pool D7 samples together. 

For the D8 sample, the column had sunk into the actual buffer exchange tube – these will either lead to total loss or poor recovery of the sample. For this sample:

### **1\. Pre-Rinse Filter**

1. Add **500 µL** nuclease-free water to the filter (upper chamber).

2. Centrifuge **10 min @ 10,000×g**.

3. Discard the filtrate (flow-through).

---

### **2\. Adjust Sample to 350 µL**

1. Transfer **200 µL** sample into a fresh low-binding tube.

2. Add **300 µL** Nucleosome Buffer C → **total \= 500 µL**.

---

### **3\. First Diafiltration**

1. Load the **500 µL** diluted sample into the filter unit.

2. Centrifuge **10 min @ 10,000×g**.

3. Discard the filtrate (contains imidazole, Tween, etc.).

---

### **4\. Wash Step**

1. Add **200 µL** fresh Streptavidin Binding Buffer to the filter unit.

2. Centrifuge **10 min @ 10,000×g**.

3. Discard the filtrate.

> *If you suspect residual imidazole, repeat this wash (step 4\) once more with another 350 µL.*

---

### **5\. Recover Buffer-Exchanged Sample**

1. Move the filter to a **new collection tube** (provided).

2. Invert the unit and spin **5 min @ 1,000×g**.

3. Discard the filter—your retentate is now in the tube.

After the recovery of sample D8, concentration of samples will be measured using Qubit:

D7: 20.2 ng/uL

D8: 2.18 ng/uL

- Place tubes on magnetic rack and allow liquid to separate from beads for 5 mins.  
- Perform two washes with 80% ethanol (200-300 uL each)  
- Allow the beads to dry for \~30s and then add fresh TE buffer in desired volume for elution. Mix thoroughly and incubate at RT for 5 mins.  
- Place tubes on magnetic rack and allow liquid to separate from beads for 5 mins. Collect separated supernatant as purified DNA sample.

**Storage Conditions** 

Plasmids are delivered in vials with red or blue caps;  Store the vials at 4°C for short-term and frequent use, or at \-20°C for long-term storage. 

**Material Instructions** 

Keep the vial sealed until ready to use. Centrifuge at 6,000 x g for 1 minute at 4°C. Open the vial and add 20 μl of sterilized water to dissolve the DNA. Close the lid and vortex the vial for 1 minute. If necessary, heat the solution at 50°C for 15 minutes to dissolve the DNA. Transformation of the plasmid DNA can be performed directly after completion of the steps above. Verify the plasmid sequence after each subcloning and transformation step.

**9 November 2025**

***1\. Lysis for new DBP protein cultures***

Lysis Mix (for 8 tubes) \= 125mL Lysis Buffer \+ 75mg lysozyme \+ 313µL PMSF (200mM in MeOH).  
Adjust buffer volume by the mass of lysozyme. Note that PMSF is toxic.  
Imidazole solution is stable for 2 years at 4℃, protected from light.

Spin cultures at 3600 rpm, 17 C, 20 min. Sequential spin to accumulate the culture and pellets in the same 50 mL falcon.

Post-addition of lysis buffer , vortex thoroughly and then shake at high speed for 30 minutes.

**12 November 2025**

**Cycle 1 NCAP-SELEX (lig147, ligN40w107)**

*Step 1: Nucleosome recon*

- 1.8 uL instead of 1.5 uL was added for the octamer per well  
- 5 uL each of 20 ng/uL ligands was used per well  
- For all aspirate & dispense steps change the distance to *‘0.2-0.5 from bottom’* \- especially to add to SBP beads

*Step 2: Nucleosome wash*

- Rinse 3 times with Water (Ch2)  
- Prime once with Water  
- Rinse once with Ch1 & Prime with 200 mL of Ch1  
- Wash lig147 plate first (6+6+3)  
- Rinse 3 times with Water (Ch2)  
- Prime once with Water  
- Rinse once with Ch1 & Prime with 200 mL of Ch1  
- Wash ligN40w107 plate first (6+6+3)

*Step 3: Biotin Buffer*

- Shake at 1900 rpm, 1.5 mins for Biotin elution  
- 10 uL of P22, P24 were collected and pooled for each ligand prior to moving sample for TF incubation

*Step 4: TF incubation*

- There is 30 uL of TF per well ⇒ Take 25 uL TF after mixing 5-6 times and then add to Master TF plate ⇒ Add 65 uL TF dilution buffer per well and mix again 5-6 times

*Step 6: DNA elution*

- Incubate at 80 C, 15 mins for DNA elution (on a 384-well plate)

*Step 6: PCR & qPCR*

qPCR: 1.5 uL DNA to qPCR plate

PCR: 4.5 uL DNA to PCR plate with 13.5 uL

PCRc2: 2 uL of PCR1 to PCR2 plate (18.5 uL)

*NCAP-SELEX process & salt concentration summary*

| *\#* | *Experimental step* | *Total volume (µL)* | *Monovalent salt (mM)* | *Key biochemical event* |
| ----- | ----- | ----- | ----- | ----- |
| ***1*** | *5 µL no-salt DNA added to dried 5 µL of 2 M salt* | ***5.0*** | ***2000 mM (2.0 M)*** | *High-salt starting point for nucleosome reconstitution.* |
| ***2*** | *\+ 1.8 µL histone octamer (1 M salt)* | ***6.8*** | ***1735 mM*** | *Histones fully soluble; nucleosome assembly precursor mix.* |
| ***3*** | *Serial no-salt additions (5 → 2.5 → 2.5 → 3.5 → 3.5 → 30 → 15 µL)* | ***68.8*** | ***1.00 → 0.83 → 0.70 → 0.58 → 0.50 → 0.22 → 0.17 M*** | *Stepwise salt gradient drives histone deposition and nucleosome formation.* |
| ***4*** | *Transfer to streptavidin beads (+ 5 µL bead buffer 0 mM)* | ***73.8*** | ***160 mM*** | *SBP-nucleosomes captured; physiological ionic strength.* |
| ***5*** | *Wash with 140 mM KCl \+ 5 mM NaCl buffer; residual ≈ 5 µL* | ***5.0*** | ***145 mM*** | *Removes unbound/weak species; retains stable nucleosomes.* |
| ***6*** | *Add 25 µL biotin \+ imidazole buffer (70 mM KCl \+ 2.5 mM NaCl) → mix 2 min* | ***30.0*** | ***84.6 mM*** | *Biotin displaces SBP-octamers; nucleosomes released into supernatant.* |
| ***7*** | *Collect **28 µL** supernatant \+ add 4.5 µL His-TF mix (150 mM)* | ***32.5*** | ***96.7 mM*** | *TF–nucleosome complexes form in solution; moderate, TF-friendly salt.* |
| ***8*** | *Transfer to Ni beads (+ 5 µL 0 mM buffer)* | ***37.5*** | ***83.9 mM*** | *His-tagged TFs (± bound nucleosomes) bind Ni; co-capture of complexes.* |
| ***9*** | *Wash with low-salt Ni buffer (70 mM KCl \+ 2.5 mM NaCl \+ 0.5 mM MgCl₂ \+ 2 mM β-ME); residual ≈ 5 µL* | ***5.0*** | ***72.5 mM*** | *Removes unbound nucleosomes, free TFs, biotin, imidazole; leaves specific complexes.* |
| ***10*** | *Add 35 µL elution buffer (0.1 % Tween, 10 mM Tris pH 7.8, 1 mM MgCl₂) \+ heat 80 °C × 15 min* | ***40.0*** | ***≈ 9 mM*** | *Heat breaks His–Ni bonds; proteins denature, nucleosomes dissociate, DNA released.* |

HT-SELEX protocol:

4.5 uL protein \+ 8.5 uL 1.55X SELEX Buffer  
Add 5 uL DNA to mix above. Incubate at RT, 30 mins.

Aspirate blocking buffer from Ni beads. Add 20 uL of 1 X SELEX Buffer. Transfer previous mix to the beads \+ 20 uL 1 X SELEX buffer.

Seal plates and incubate 1900 rpm, 45 mins.

Wash and then add 35 uL of elution buffer per well. Heat plates at 80 degrees, 15 minutes to elute out DNA.

Add 135μL \* 96well in AB0765.

**Clean-Up of SELEX libraries for AVITI sequencing**

Briefly:

- For each library I take 1 mL of the sample and concentrate this in the SpeedVac for about \~2 hours  
- Then perform size selection as shown below  
- Dilute the samples 100-fold and then use 2 uL of each to run on a TapeStation HSD5000

*SPRIselect Bead-Based Size Selection Protocol for Retaining 100-400 bp Fragments and Removing \>500 bp Fragments*  
*(Modified with a 0.75× ratio for better exclusion of larger fragments)*  
   
This protocol is optimized for **SPRIselect beads** (Beckman Coulter) to selectively retain **100-400 bp DNA fragments**, while removing **fragments \>500 bp**, using a higher bead-to-sample ratio (**0.7× instead of 0.6×**) to improve exclusion of large fragments.  
   
**Materials Needed**

 	•       	**SPRIselect beads** (Beckman Coulter, B23317)

 	•       	**Freshly prepared 80% ethanol (molecular biology grade)**

 	•       	**10 mM Tris-HCl, pH 8.0 or nuclease-free water**

 	•       	**Magnetic stand for bead separation**

 	•       	**Pipettes and tips**

 	•       	**DNA sample (e.g., PCR product, fragmented DNA library)**

 	•       	**0.2 mL or 1.5 mL tubes (depending on sample volume)**  
 

**Protocol Overview**  
   
This protocol consists of a **two-step size selection**:

    	1\.  	**First selection (high cutoff step)** – Removes large fragments (\>500 bp).

    	2\.  	**Second selection (low cutoff step)** – Retains the desired 100-400 bp range while removing fragments \<100 bp.  
   
**Step-by-Step Protocol**  
   
**Step 1: First Bead Selection to Remove Large Fragments (\>500 bp)**

    	1\.  	**Resuspend SPRIselect beads** by vortexing or pipetting thoroughly.

    	2\.  	In a clean PCR or 1.5 mL tube, mix your **DNA sample** with **SPRIselect beads at a 0.75× bead-to-sample ratio**:

         	•	Example: For **50 µL of DNA sample**, add **35 µL of SPRIselect beads**.

    	3\.  	Mix thoroughly by pipetting up and down or vortexing briefly.

    	4\.  	Incubate at **room temperature for 5-10 minutes** to allow binding.

    	5\.  	Place the tube on a **magnetic stand** for **5 minutes**, or until the solution clears.

    	6\.  	**Carefully transfer the supernatant** (which contains fragments \<500 bp) to a new clean tube. **Do not disturb the beads**, as they contain larger fragments bound to them.

    	7\.  	Discard the beads containing the \>500 bp fragments.  
   
**Step 2: Second Bead Selection to Remove Small Fragments (\<100 bp)**

        	8\.   Add **(1.8x – 0.75x) SPRIselect beads** to the collected supernatant:

         	•	Example: If you have **50 µL of supernatant**, add **55 µL of SPRIselect beads**.

        	9\.   Mix thoroughly by pipetting up and down or vortexing briefly.

        	10\.              	Incubate at **room temperature for 5-10 minutes**.

        	11\.              	Place the tube on a **magnetic stand** for **5 minutes**, or until the solution clears.

        	12\.              	**Carefully remove and discard the supernatant**, which contains unwanted fragments \<100 bp.

        	13\.              	Keeping the tube on the magnetic stand, **add 200 µL of freshly prepared 85% ethanol**, avoiding bead resuspension.

        	14\.              	Incubate for **30 seconds**, then carefully remove the ethanol.

        	15\.              	**Repeat Steps 13-14** for a **second ethanol wash**.

        	16\.              	**Air-dry the beads** for **2-3 minutes** (do not over-dry, as it may reduce DNA recovery).

        	17\.              	Remove the tube from the magnetic stand and **resuspend the beads in 20-50 µL of 10 mM Tris-HCl (pH 8.0) or nuclease-free water**.

        	18\.              	Incubate at **room temperature for 5 minutes**.

        	19\.              	Place the tube back on the **magnetic stand** for 1-2 minutes.

        	20\.              	**Transfer the clear eluate** to a new tube—this contains your size-selected DNA (100-400 bp).  
   
**Quality Control**

 	•       	**Assess DNA fragment size distribution** using an **Agilent Bioanalyzer, TapeStation, or gel electrophoresis**.

 	•       	**Measure DNA concentration** using a **Qubit fluorometer or Nanodrop spectrophotometer**.

 	•       	**Confirm fragment size enrichment** before proceeding to downstream applications.  
   
**Expected Results**

 	•       	**Retained DNA fragments:** **100-400 bp**

 	•       	**Removed fragments:** **\>500 bp (high molecular weight contaminants) and \<100 bp (adapter dimers, primer-dimers, or short degradation products)**  
   
**Notes & Optimizations**

 	•       	The **0.7× ratio** increases the likelihood of **excluding larger fragments (\>500 bp)** but may slightly reduce yield.

 	•       	If small fragments persist, increasing the **second bead selection ratio** may help remove more unwanted \<100 bp fragments.

 	•       	Avoid overdrying the beads during ethanol washes to prevent low DNA recovery.

 	•       	Ensure beads are fully resuspended before use for consistency.

Additional QC is performed by running:

1 uL of each sample post cleanup is run on a 2% agarose gel and then stained with SYBR Green.

Repeat a cleanup with about 150 uL of the fresh sample:

0.85-0.70

- Add 105 uL (0.7X) beads to each sample. Incubate 10 mins, RT. Bind to magnet for 5 mins.  
- Move supernatant to new tubes and then add (150 x (0.85 \- 0.7) \= 22.5 uL). Incubate 10 mins at RT. Bind for 5 mins to magnet.  
- Remove supernatant and wash twice with 200 uL 85% ethanol.  
- Elute each sample in 50 uL of TE buffer.  
- Run Qubit and TS

Target concentration is around 50 nM for each of the samples. No stringent volume requirement. Loading based on the last successful run on the AVITI was 15.4 pM of each pool was loaded.

Samples were all diluted 1000-fold.

Pool the PCR product and run 20 uL of sample on a 1% agarose gel. Measure concentration of the PCR1 product.

Upon running this PCR product on a 2% agarose gel no product was seen.

This suggested too much dilution of the initial annealed PCR product. 

I then ran another PCR with just 10-fold dilution of the original annealed product and this yielded a product of the right size but also retained the shorter annealed fragments.

**13 January 2026**

*lig70N PCR*

To address the PCR issues for lig70N further, I am now gel extracting the lig70N DNA and then setting up a PCR to check if the product is cleaner post this PCR.

This resolved the issue as a very clean band was obtained post PCR1 in this case.

Further, two conditions had been tried for PCR here:

- dilution of original lig70N ANN 10-fold → use 1 uL in PCR  
- dilution of original lig70N ANN 15-fold → use 1 uL in PCR

The first condition with 10-fold dilution yielded a better overall concentration of the product.

Basically, I reconsitute 20ul nucleosome with 4ug DNA ligand, then I add 20x molar ratio of ELF2 to the nucleosome (not the whole 20ul, just the amount needed from EcoRV digestion, the rest I keep at 4deg for up to a month), incubate at 25deg fro 15min, then add cutsmart buffer & EcoRV & incubate at 37deg fro 15min. Then I add a mixture of SDS+proteinase K to stop the digestion (detail see the attached file), and incubate at 55deg for 10min. Before running the gel, I purify the DNA with the qiagen PCR purification kit (so the gel looks nicer) and load 100-200ng DNA onto a 3% agarose gel and run on ice (I found out that the gel turns out quite ugly if I load more than 200ng DNA).

- Overnight ligation \+ QC of long-read DNA

*HTFS ligation trial \- purification & QC*

Perform clean-up with Ampure XP beads:

- Add 1.5X volume of AmpureXP beads and mix thoroughly. Incubate at RT for 15 mins.  
- Place tubes on magnetic rack and allow liquid to separate from beads for 5 mins.  
- Perform two washes with 80% ethanol (200-300 uL each)  
- Allow the beads to dry for \~30s and then add fresh TE buffer in desired volume for elution. Mix thoroughly and incubate at RT for 5 mins.  
- Place tubes on magnetic rack and allow liquid to separate from beads for 5 mins. Collect separated supernatant as purified DNA sample.

*ELF ligand nucleosome reconstitution*

*Basically, I reconsitute 20ul nucleosome with 4ug DNA ligand, then I add 20x molar ratio of ELF2 to the nucleosome (not the whole 20ul, just the amount needed from EcoRV digestion, the rest I keep at 4deg for up to a month), incubate at 25deg fro 15min, then add cutsmart buffer & EcoRV & incubate at 37deg fro 15min. Then I add a mixture of SDS+proteinase K to stop the digestion (detail see the attached file), and incubate at 55deg for 10min. Before running the gel, I purify the DNA with the qiagen PCR purification kit (so the gel looks nicer) and load 100-200ng DNA onto a 3% agarose gel and run on ice (I found out that the gel turns out quite ugly if I load more than 200ng DNA).* 

10 uL of 4 M KCl was dried in tubes.

Resuspend in 20 uL of DNA

1. ELF2\_dm\_EcoRV  
2. ELF2\_sm\_EcorRV  
3. ELF2\_sm2\_EcoRV  
4. ELF2\_dm\_noEcoRV

Take 4 ug of each DNA.

Molarity of DNA \= 1.68 uM

Molarity of octamer required \= 1.52 uM ⇒ 4.14 ug

Octamer concentration \= 5.4 ug/uL

Prepare 828 ng/uL stock (22 uL) ⇒ 3.37 uL of WT-octamer \+ 18.63 refolding buffer:glycerol ⇒ Salt conc. 1.15 M

Add 5 uL of this stock to each tube of the DNA \+ salt ⇒ Salt conc \= 1.83 M

*Check ligation product integrity*

#### **Protein stripping (Proteinase K \+ SDS)**

In a 10 µL reaction:

* DNA: 25 ng

* **SDS 10%**: **0.1 µL** (final 0.1%)  
* **Proteinase K (20 mg/mL)**: **0.2 µL** (final \~0.4 mg/mL)

Incubate:

* **55°C, 20 min**

* then **70°C, 10 min** (helps inactivate Proteinase K and relax complexes)

*Bradford assay for ELF2 and ELF2 mutant 3 protein estimation*

Proteins to be measured:

ELF2 WT (new)

ELF2 WT DBD → from Q3 DBD (Well E4)

ELF2 mutant 3

![][image6]

1\. Pipette 5 µL of each standard or unknown sample into the appropriate microplate wells (e.g., Thermo Scientific™ Pierce™ 96-Well Plates, Cat. No. 15041). 

2\. Add 250 µL of the Bradford reagent to each well, then mix with plate shaker for 30 seconds. 

3\. Remove the plate from the shaker. 

4\. Incubate the plate for 10 minutes at RT. 

5\. Measure the absorbance at or near 595 nm with a plate reader. 

6\. Subtract the average 595 nm measurement for the blank replicates from the 595 nm measurements of all other individual standard and unknown sample replicates. Pierce™ Bradford Protein Assay Kit User Guide 3

7\. Prepare a standard curve by plotting the average blank-corrected 595 nm measurement for each BSA standard vs. its concentration in µg/mL. Use the standard curve to determine the protein concentration of each unknown sample. Note: If using curve-fitting algorithms associated with a microplate reader, a four-parameter (quadratic) or best-fit curve will provide more accurate results than a purely linear fit. If plotting results by hand, a point-to-point curve is preferable to a linear fit to the standard points.

### **Nucleosome amount per reaction**

**150 ng nucleosome**

### **TF needed (20× molar excess)**

From earlier (nucleosome MW ≈ 205,845 g/mol; TF MW \= 16.05 kDa):

* 150 ng nucleosome \= **0.729 pmol**

* 20× TF \= **14.6 pmol**

* TF mass \= 14.6 pmol × 16.05 ng/pmol \= **234 ng TF per reaction**

| 1 | 55.6 | **2.70** |
| 2 | 30.2 | **4.97** |
| 3 | 43.2 | **3.47** |
| 4 | 54.2 | **2.77** |

Dilute TFs using 50 mM TF dilution buffer:

### **Resulting TF concentrations**

* ELF2-2 stock: 477.855 ng/µL → /4 \= **119.46 ng/µL** (4 uL original protein \+ 12 uL 50 mM buffer)

* ELF2m3 stock: 397.935 ng/µL → /4 \= **99.48 ng/µL** (4 uL original protein \+ 12 uL 50 mM buffer)

  Both at **75 mM salt**.

### **TF volumes needed to deliver 234 ng**

* **ELF2-2:** 234 / 119.46 \= **1.96 µL**

* **ELF2m3:** 234 / 99.48 \= **2.35 µL**

*SELEX checklist*

1\. Ligand plates

lig200 \- Prepare 15 X 400 \= 6000 uL of stock \[Requires 15 X 20 X 400 \= 120 ug DNA\]

For each ligand split into 62.5 uL per well of a 96-well storage plate. From here aliquot evenly (15 uL per well) to NUNC 384-well plates. Store these ligand plates after labelling at \-20.

2\. Biotin Buffer plates

200 mL

| 1X TCAPT Block | 100 mL |
| :---- | :---- |
| Biotin | 0.49 g |
| Imidazole | 0.68 g |
| 10 M NaOH | 120 uL |

Make up the volume to 200 mL. DO NOT filter and aliquot 25 uL per well to 12 plates (needs \~120 mL buffer).

*Clean-up ELFlig1 nucleosomes (removal of free DNA population)*

### **Pre-Rinse Filter**

Add **500 µL** nuclease-free water to the filter (upper chamber) of a 100 kDa column.  
Centrifuge **10 min @ 14,000×g**.  
Discard the filtrate (flow-through).

**Adjust Sample to 200 µL**

Transfer your **200 µL** sample into a fresh low-binding tube.

**First Diafiltration**

Load the **200 µL** sample into the filter unit.  
Centrifuge **10 min @ 14,000×g**.  
Store the filtrate (contains imidazole, Tween, etc.) so that it can be checked for any remaining DNA, protein.

**Wash Step**

Add **200 µL** fresh Nucleosome Exchange Buffer E to the filter unit.  
Centrifuge **10 min @ 14,000×g**.  
Store the wash.

**Recover Buffer-Exchanged Sample**

Move the filter to a **new collection tube** (provided).  
Invert the unit and spin **5 min @ 1,500×g**.  
Discard the filter—your retentate is now in the tube.

The following tubes will now be generated based on these purifications:

| Sample | ng/uL |
| ----- | :---: |
| lig1\_FT | N/A |
| lig1\_W | N/A |
| lig1\_E | 5.62 |

MNase treatment was then performed for ELFlig1 and the concentrated/cleaned-up ELFlig1nuc sample.

Run these samples on a 3% agarose gel:

- ELFlig1  
- ELFlig1-MNase  
- ELFlig1nuc-MNase

*Plasmid FIBER-Seq (HAP1)*

1. Cells are resuspended in 60 uL of Hia5 buffer (containing 0.05 % IGEPAL)  
2. Incubate on ice for 10 mins.  
3. Pellet nuclei at 350g, 5 min at 4 C.  
4. Resuspend nuclei in 60 uL Hia5 buffer (with 0.8 mM SAM) & 0.5 uL of Hia5  
5. Incubate at 25 C for 40 mins. Add SDS to a final concentration of 1% to stop the reaction.  
6. Extract plasmid DNA by adding 137 uL Monarch Buffer B1. Then purify by mini-prep according to the kit instructions.  
7. Linearize the plasmids based on *in vitro* CRISPR & clean-up the reactions

*Transfer reconstituted nucleosomes to lower salt buffer*

Take 1 mini-dialysis column labeled F1 (293T20knuc). Equilibrate the column with Nucleosome Buffer C for 30 mins. *SBP Histone Octamer Plate*

Octamer concentration \= 7.25 ug/uL \= 7250 ng/uL

Number of experiments \= 12

Volume per experiment \= 1.8 uL

Volume needed per well \= 22 uL

Prepare 30 ng/uL octamer with 25 uL per well ⇒ 25 X 400 X 35 \= 300 ug SBP-octamer

Take 41.4 uL of original octamer and make up solution to 10000 uL (add 9958.6 uL of refolding buffer:glycerol (1:1))

Add 833 uL per well of a 96-well storage plate. From here use the Beckman robot to first add 104 uL per well of same 96-well plate & then finally aliquot this to 25 uL per well in a single 4ti384-well plate.

HT-SELEX Protocol Summary  
4.5 uL protein \+ 8.5 uL 1.55X SELEX Buffer  
Add 5 uL DNA to mix above. Incubate at RT, 30 mins.

Aspirate blocking buffer from Ni beads. Add 20 uL of 1 X SELEX Buffer. Transfer previous mix to the beads \+ 20 uL 1 X SELEX buffer.

Seal plates and incubate 1900 rpm, 45 mins.

Wash and then add 35 uL of elution buffer per well. Heat plates at 80 degrees, 15 minutes to elute out DNA.

*NCAP & HT SELEX \- lig147 & ligN40w107*

The qPCR was run for these samples with both the NCAP-SELEX and HT-SELEX samples:

Quadrant 1 NCAP lig147 c1 → Quadrant 1 qPCR plate

Quadrant 2 NCAP ligN40 c1 → Quadrant 2 qPCR plate

Quadrant 3 HTS lig147 c1 → Quadrant 3 qPCR plate

Quadrant 4 HTS ligN40 c1 → Quadrant 4 qPCR plate

| Sample | Mean Value | Median Value |
| :---: | :---: | :---: |
| NCAP\_lig147\_c1 | 32 | 36 |
| NCAP\_ligN40\_c1 | 35 | 38 |
| HTS\_lig147\_c1 | 22 | 29 |
| HTS\_ligN40\_c1 | 33 | 36 |

Based on the qPCR results, it seems like there was actually no signal in the majority of the cells. Upon checking the Beckman program again, what we can see is that the BC90 tips are not at all good at picking up small volumes during the qPCR assay and depositing them into the qPCR plate.

Therefore, this needs to be repeated with the BC384 tips, split into 4 quadrants.

Set up PCR1 according to the following cycle numbers:

NCAP\_lig147\_c1 \=\> 32  
NCAP\_ligN40\_c1 \=\> 35

**30 March 2026**

*SELEX library preparation and QC*

Repeat a cleanup with about 300 uL of the fresh sample:

1.1-0.70

- Add 210 uL (0.7X) beads to each sample. Incubate 5 mins, RT. Bind to magnet for 5 mins.  
- Move supernatant to new tubes and then add (300 x (1.1 \- 0.7) \= 120 uL). Incubate 5 mins at RT. Bind for 5 mins to magnet.  
- Remove supernatant and wash twice with 300 uL 85% ethanol. Air dry for 2-3 mins.  
- Elute each sample in 75 uL of TE buffer.  
- Run Qubit and TS (D5000)

*Plasmid FIBER-Seq GP5d (pHRS)*

- Cells are resuspended in 60 uL of Hia5 buffer (containing 0.05 % IGEPAL)  
- Incubate on ice for 10 mins.  
- Pellet nuclei at 350g, 5 min at 4 C.  
- Resuspend nuclei in 58.5 uL Hia5 buffer (with 0.8 mM SAM). During this step resuspend all the nuclei in 58.5 uL (with SAM) for the same time point. Then add 1.5 uL of Hia5.  
- Incubate at 25 C for 60 mins. Add SDS to a final concentration of 1% to stop the reaction.  
- Extract plasmid DNA by adding 134 uL Monarch Buffer B1. Then purify by mini-prep according to the kit instructions.  
- Linearize the plasmids based on *in vitro* CRISPR & clean-up the reactions

*HTFS & NCFS reaction set up*

HTFS

Reaction Volume \= 20 uL

For 1000 wells (2 ligands):

Total \= 2 x 1000 \= 2000 uL  
Hia5 \= 0.01 x 1000 \= 10 uL  
SAM \= 0.5 x 1000 \= 500 uL  
Hia5 buffer \= 1490 uL

Split into 20 uL per well of a 96 well plate and then add 2 uL to each well of the HTFS plates (total needed \= 16 uL).

NCFS

Reaction Volume \= 35 uL  
For 1000 wells (2 ligands):

Total \= 3 x 1000 \= 3000 uL  
Hia5 \= 0.01 x 1000 \= 10 uL  
SAM \= 0.9 x 1000 \= 900 uL  
Hia5 buffer \= 2090 uL

Split into 31 uL per well of 96-well plate and then add 3 uL per well of the NCFS plates (total needed \= 24 uL).

NOTES:

- I have reduced the amount of enzyme used to 0.01 uL per well of a SELEX reaction  
- For the Hia5 treatment, I first incubated at 37 C for \~40 min, and then changed to incubation at 25 C for 1 hour → the idea behind this was that since the TF-DNA interactions were set up at RT, it might be better to carry out the foot-printing as well at 25 C

*HTFS, NCFS ligation purification*

Proteinase K \+ SDS treatment

* **Make up value to 100 uL with TE buffer**  
* **SDS 10%**: **2 µL** (final 0.2%)  
* **Proteinase K (20 mg/mL)**: **2 µL** (final \~0.4 mg/mL)

Incubate at RT, 15-30 mins.

- Add 1.5X volume of AmpureXP beads and mix thoroughly. Incubate at RT for 10 mins.  
- Place tubes on magnetic rack and allow liquid to separate from beads for 5 mins.  
- Perform two washes with 80% ethanol (200-300 uL each)  
- Allow the beads to dry for \~30s and then add fresh TE buffer in 40 uL for elution. Mix thoroughly and incubate at 37 C for 5 mins.  
- Place tubes on magnetic rack and allow liquid to separate from beads for 5 mins. Collect separated supernatant as purified DNA sample.  
- Repeat last two steps with a smaller volume of 10 uL for improved yield.

*AVITI Sequencing*

I have normally been pooling C1+C3+C4 samples in one flow cell for sequencing. While this works pretty well, I can clearly see lower reads for C3, C4 samples (This used 4 flow cells \- 1 FC per ligand (HT-SELEX \+ NCAP-SELEX))

Now, 6 flow-cells will be used instead for sequencing the following samples:

* C1\_HTS\_lig200 (PE0) \+ C1\_HTS\_lig70N (PE1)  
* C1\_NCAP\_lig200 (PE0) \+ C1\_NCAP\_lig70N (PE1)  
* C3\_HTS\_lig200 (PE0) \+ C3\_HTS\_lig70N (PE1)  
* C3\_NCAP\_lig200 (PE2) \+ C3\_NCAP\_lig70N (PE3)  
* C1\_HTS\_lig200 (PE2) \+ C1\_HTS\_lig70N (PE3)  
* C1\_NCAP\_lig200 (PE2) \+ C1\_NCAP\_lig70N (PE3)

Now, 6 flow-cells will be used instead for sequencing the following samples:

* C1\_HTS\_lig200 (PE0) \+ C1\_NCAP\_lig200 (PE1)  
* C1\_HTS\_lig70N (PE0) \+ C1\_NCAP\_lig70N (PE1)  
* C3\_HTS\_lig200 (PE2) \+ C3\_NCAP\_lig200 (PE3)  
* C3\_HTS\_lig70N (PE2) \+ C3\_NCAP\_lig70N (PE3)  
* C4\_HTS\_lig200 (PE2) \+ C4\_NCAP\_lig200 (PE3)   
* C4\_HTS\_lig70N (PE2) \+ C4\_NCAP\_lig70N (PE3)

*SELEX library preparation and QC*

Cleanup with about 500 uL of the fresh sample:

1.1-0.70

- Add 350 uL (0.7X) beads to each sample. Incubate 5 mins, RT. Bind to magnet for 5 mins.  
- Move supernatant to new tubes and then add (500 x (1.1 \- 0.7) \= 200 uL). Incubate 5 mins at RT. Bind for 5 mins to magnet.  
- Remove supernatant and wash twice with 300 uL 85% ethanol. Air dry for 2-3 mins.  
- Elute each sample in 75 uL of TE buffer.  
- Run Qubit and TS (D5000)

*SELEX library QC*

For the KAPA kit:

Dilute each sample 1:10000 and then load 4 uL of the diluted sample.

**12 May 2026**

*HTFS, NCFS ligation purification*

Proteinase K \+ SDS treatment

* **Make up value to 100 uL with TE buffer**  
* **SDS 10%**: **2 µL** (final 0.2%)  
* **Proteinase K (20 mg/mL)**: **2 µL** (final \~0.4 mg/mL)

Incubate at RT, 15-30 mins.

- Add 1.5X volume of AmpureXP beads and mix thoroughly. Incubate at RT for 10 mins.  
- Place tubes on magnetic rack and allow liquid to separate from beads for 5 mins.  
- Perform two washes with 80% ethanol (200-300 uL each)  
- Allow the beads to dry for \~30s and then add fresh TE buffer in 40 uL for elution. Mix thoroughly and incubate at 37 C for 5 mins.  
- Place tubes on magnetic rack and allow liquid to separate from beads for 5 mins. Collect separated supernatant as purified DNA sample.  
- Repeat last two steps with a smaller volume of 10 uL for improved yield.

***SELEX library clean-up***

Cleanup with about 600 uL of the fresh sample:

1.1-0.70

- Add 420 uL (0.7X) beads to each sample. Incubate 5 mins, RT (with nutation). Bind to magnet for 5 mins.  
- Move supernatant to new tubes and then add (600 x (1.1 \- 0.7) \= 240 uL). Incubate 5 mins at RT (with nutation). Bind for 5 mins to magnet.  
- Remove supernatant and wash twice with 300 uL 85% ethanol. Air dry for 2-3 mins.  
- Elute each sample in 100 uL of TE buffer.  
- Run Qubit and TS (D5000)

- 23 uL DNA \+ 18.5 uL undiluted ELF2 \+ 41.5 uL 1.55X SELEX Buffer \[\~93 mM salt, \~14% glycerol\]

Proteinase K \+ SDS treatment

* **Make up value to 100 uL with TE buffer**  
* **SDS 10%**: **2 µL** (final 0.2%)  
* **Proteinase K (20 mg/mL)**: **2 µL** (final \~0.4 mg/mL)

Incubate at RT, 15-30 mins.

- Add 1.5X volume of AmpureXP beads and mix thoroughly. Incubate at RT for 10 mins.  
- Place tubes on magnetic rack and allow liquid to separate from beads for 5 mins.  
- Perform two washes with 80% ethanol (200-300 uL each)  
- Allow the beads to dry for \~30s and then add fresh TE buffer in 40 uL for elution. Mix thoroughly and incubate at 37 C for 5 mins.  
- Place tubes on magnetic rack and allow liquid to separate from beads for 5 mins. Collect separated supernatant as purified DNA sample.  
- Repeat last two steps with a smaller volume of 10 uL for improved yield.

Hia5 buffer  
*Hia5*  
Sample

Two samples:

1. ligYJnuc

Set up two reaction per sample:

| Component | Volume | Final condition |
| ----- | ----- | ----- |
| Complete Hia5 Buffer A | 21.25 µL minus sample volume | 1× |
| 32 mM SAM | 1.25 µL | 0.8 mM |

Salt concentration during the reaction ⇒ 72-78 mM salt

- Place tubes on magnetic rack and allow liquid to separate from beads for 5 mins.  
- Perform two washes with 80% ethanol (200-300 uL each)  
- Allow the beads to dry for \~30s and then add fresh TE buffer in desired volume for elution. Mix thoroughly and incubate at RT for 5 mins.  
- Place tubes on magnetic rack and allow liquid to separate from beads for 5 mins. Collect separated supernatant as purified DNA sample.

*Purification of TdT reactions*

- To each subpool, add SDS to a final concentration of 0.08%   
- Then do a 1:1 ampure bead cleanup following the standard ampure cleanup protocol. Because the solution has SDS in it, do not mix vigorously (this will create bubbles).   
- Elute in 40ul NF warm water   
- Measure the concentration on the nanodrop using the ssDNA function. 

Concentration obtained \= \~102 ng/uL ssDNA

Expected yield for about 50 uL based on the original protocol is \~11 ug. The current total yield is \~4-5 ug so far lower than we typically expect.

To prepare 100x SYBR Green: add 5μL 10,000x SYBR Green into 500μL DMSO, which can be found in Phusion DNA Pol kits. Avoid light. Manually load 122μL mix into each well of a 96-well plate (AB0765), then transfer 4.5μL/well into 6x 384-well plates (LightCycler 384).

*Nucleosome Reconstitution*

Yujiao’s long random DNA  
DNA → 4 ug in 25 uL → 24.7 nM  
Octamer:DNA ratio \= 50:1  
WT Octamer (5.4 mg/mL) → 1.25 uM →  3.4 ug ⇒ 0.68 ug/uL octamer stock \[0.76 uL octamer \+ 5.24 uL refolding buffer:glycerol\] ⇒ Octamer stock salt conc \= 1.13 M  
1.83 M salt in octamer-DNA mix.

- First incubate the dialysis column in cold NF-water to remove any glycerol from the column  
- Add the 25 uL of octamer-DNA mix to the dialysis column

*PEG solution*

Weigh 5 g PEG8000 and dissolve in 10 mL sterile water \- heat at 50-60 C because PEG8000 takes a long time to dissolve.

Proteinase K \+ SDS treatment

* **Make up value to 100 uL with TE buffer**  
* **SDS 10%**: **2 µL** (final 0.2%)  
* **Proteinase K (20 mg/mL)**: **2 µL** (final \~0.4 mg/mL)

Incubate at RT, 15-30 mins.

- Add 1.5X volume of AmpureXP beads and mix thoroughly. Incubate at RT for 10 mins.  
- Place tubes on magnetic rack and allow liquid to separate from beads for 5 mins.  
- Perform two washes with 80% ethanol (200-300 uL each)  
- Allow the beads to dry for \~30s and then add fresh TE buffer in 40 uL for elution. Mix thoroughly and incubate at 37 C for 5 mins.  
- Place tubes on magnetic rack and allow liquid to separate from beads for 5 mins. Collect separated supernatant as purified DNA sample.  
- Repeat last two steps with a smaller volume of 10 uL for improved yield.

*MNase test for new nucleosome samples* 

15 ng of sample (0.5 uL) \+ 5 uL MNase buffer \+ 1 uL dMNase \+ 0.25 uL rAlbumin (total 50 uL)

dMNase → 1:1000 dilution

Treat at 37 C, 5 mins.

Run on a 1% agarose gel and stain with SYBR Green.

0.9-0.6

9 October 2026

Yimeng’s paper nucleosome recon protocol:

- Starts with 628.9 nM of dsDNA (lig147)

The dsDNA ligands were assembled onto histone octamers containing streptavidin binding protein (SBP)-tagged H2A proteins by stepwise reduction of salt concentration. Specifically, 300 ng of ligands were mixed with H2A-tagged octamers at a 1:2 molar ratio in 5 µL of 2 M NaCl solution. After 30 min incubation at room temperature, the mixture was diluted by sequential addition of TE buffer (2.5 µL, 2.5 µL, 2.5 µL, 2.5 µL, 2.5 µL, 32.5 µL, and 50 µL) supplemented with 1 mM dithiothreitol and 1× cOmplete™ EDTA-free protease inhibitor cocktail (Sigma-Aldrich), with a 1-h incubation after each addition. 

Yimeng’s protocol → DNA:Octamer Ratio \= 2:1\* 

My current protocol uses 149.74 nM DNA (lig147) & \~74 nM octamer → (DNA:Octamer Ratio \= 2:1)

Fangjie used 190.6 nM DNA (lig147) & \~58.13 nM octamer → (DNA:Octamer Ratio \= 3.2:1)

*Reconstitution of Long Random DNA fragments*
